- **specsfy-04-validate**: Use quando o usuário pede para validar, revisar, auditar ou checar se `specs/{id}-{slug}/spec.md` segue o formato rígido Specsfy/2.0 e está pronto para planejar. Use quando o usuário pedir para validar, revisar, auditar ou checar uma spec, quando uma transição automática pedir prova do Definition Gate, nova validação após correção ou aceite da entrega. Não decide requisitos nem cria checklist, relatório ou plan.md: lacunas de decisão vão para specsfy-02-backlog, correções de spec aprovada para specsfy-update-spec e o planejamento para specsfy-05-tasks. Use quando o usuário quiser quebrar a especificação em tarefas, preencher ou atualizar a seção 14. Tarefas, ordenar dependências, planejar fatias verticais ou preparar a execução, e quando uma transição automática pedir planejamento, replanejamento ou retomada após RED. Edita somente o plano dentro da fonte única: não cria tasks.md, não escreve código e não marca trabalho como concluído. Testes e RED ficam com specsfy-06-tdd-bdd e a execução com specsfy-07-implem… ([promovaweb/specsfy](https://github.com/promovaweb/specsfy/tree/HEAD/skills/specsfy-05-tasks) · ★80)
- **specsfy-06-tdd-bdd**: Use quando o usuário pede TDD, BDD, testes derivados do Gherkin de referência da especificação, rastreabilidade entre `spec.md` e testes, ou um ciclo RED-GREEN-REFACTOR. Use também quando uma transição automática pedir materialização, RED ou verificação de testes. Use para editar as seções 11–13 e executar testes; `specsfy-07-implement` continua responsável pelas tarefas de produção na seção 14 do mesmo arquivo. - **specsfy-06-tdd-bdd**: Usa o Gherkin de referência da spec para escrever testes TDD executáveis, provar RED antes da implementação, conduzir RED-GREEN-REFACTOR e auditar a rastreabilidade entre spec.md e testes nas seções 11 a 13. Use quando o usuário pedir TDD, BDD, testes derivados da especificação, rastreabilidade ou um ciclo RED-GREEN-REFACTOR, e quando uma transição automática pedir materialização, RED ou verificação de testes. - **specsfy-07-implement**: Use quando o usuário pede para implementar, executar ou concluir tarefas da seção `14. Tarefas` de `specs/{id}-{slug}/spec.md`, criar o código definido pela fonte única, continuar a próxima tarefa pronta ou finalizar a feature. Use também quando uma transição automática iniciar ou retomar a entrega. Use para produção; para apenas criar testes use specsfy-06-tdd-bdd, e para apenas decompor use specsfy-05-tasks. - **specsfy-07-implement**: Executa as tarefas da seção `14. Tarefas` de `spec.md` da feature em ordem de dependência, escreve o código de produção a partir de testes TDD em RED, registra a comprovação na própria spec e fecha o Delivery Gate. Use quando o usuário pedir para implementar, executar, continuar a próxima tarefa pronta ou concluir uma feature já planejada, ou quando uma transição automática iniciar ou retomar a entrega. Não use para apenas criar testes (use specsfy-06-tdd-bdd), apenas decompor tarefas (use specsfy-05-tasks) ou mudar o comportamento acordado (use specsfy-update-spec). - **spectral-library-matching-and-ranking**: Use when when you have discovered Mass2Motifs from MS2LDA topic modeling and need to automatically annotate them by finding the most structurally similar known spectra in a reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-and-ranking) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-and-scoring**: Use when when you have extracted a mean or ROI spectrum from MSI data (via centroid or profile mode conversion) and need to identify the biochemical composition by comparing against curated reference libraries such as LIPID MAPS, HMDB, or a custom metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-and-scoring) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-and-scoring**: Use when when you have MS/MS fragment spectra (in .mgf format) acquired from unknown metabolite features and need to annotate them against known compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spectral-library-matching-and-scoring) · ★15)
- **spectral-library-matching-annotation**: Use when you have MS2 spectral data (precursor m/z, retention time, and fragment ion patterns) from UPLC-HRMS analysis of environmental or biological samples and need to assign compound identities by comparing against known reference spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-annotation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-evaluation**: Use when you have a trained spectral embedding model (e.g., MSBERT, Spec2Vec) and need to benchmark its library matching accuracy against reference spectra on a test dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-evaluation) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-gnps**: Use when you have MS2 product-ion spectra in open formats (.mzML or .mzXML) from public mass spectrometry datasets (e.g., from MassIVE with a valid accession) and need to identify chemical compounds by comparing fragmentation patterns against the GNPS reference spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-gnps) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-ripp**: Use when you have tandem mass spectrometry data (LC-MS/MS in MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) and genomic data from a target organism, and you want to identify RiPPs by matching experimental spectra against a database of predicted post-translationally modified RiPP structures derived from. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-ripp) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-validation**: Use when you have GC-MS data preprocessed into a structured spread format and need to confirm that a set of known or suspected compounds are correctly identified in your samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-validation) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-with-cosine-similarity**: Use when you have a query spectrum and a reduced candidate set of library spectra (via approximate nearest neighbor indexing), and need to search for exact unmodified peptide matches with high confidence before proceeding to variable-mass-shift modification searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-with-cosine-similarity) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-matching-with-taxonomy**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra (.mgf) and candidate metabolite annotations (with m/z, retention time, chemical identifiers) linked to a known organism or taxon, and you want to rank annotations by both spectral similarity AND biochemical likelihood in that organism's lineage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-matching-with-taxonomy) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **spectral-library-merging-and-deduplication**: Use when when building a comprehensive reference spectral library for metabolomics or chemical identification, you have multiple source libraries in different formats (msp, mgf, NIST binary) and ionization modes (positive/negative MS/MS or EI) that need to be combined into a single. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-merging-and-deduplication) · ★15 · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-metadata-assignment**: Use when you have a compiled EI or MS2 library object (read from MSP format via read_lib) and access to NIST ri.dat and USER.DBU files; you need to populate RI values for capillary GC-MS workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-assignment) · ★15 · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-metadata-reorganization**: Use when when ingesting mass spectral libraries (EI or MS2) where SMILES information is embedded in the Comment field rather than in a dedicated SMILES metadata field—particularly common in MoNA GC-MS and LC-MS/MS exports. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-metadata-reorganization) · ★15 · 3 copies)
- **spectral-library-molecular-networking**: Use when you have deconvolved GC-MS spectra (from overlapping chromatographic peaks) in MGF or mzTab format and want to group chemically related compounds, visualize their similarity relationships, and identify spectral families without prior library matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-molecular-networking) · ★15 · 4 copies)
- **spectral-library-msp-serialization**: Use when after generating in-memory lipid spectra (with m/z, intensity, and metadata such as lipid class, fatty acid composition, and adduct type) when you need to export those spectra as a reusable MSP-format spectral library for downstream identification tasks in Excalibur, Skyline, or NIST MS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-library-msp-serialization) · ★15 · 2 copies)
